# Vtables named by ruling (docs/DECISIONS.md D14): `<vtable symbol> <vtable address>
# <class name> derived ctor <function> [evidence...]`. The address is a fact: the
# constructor named stores the vtable's address point (address + 0x10) into its
# object, which tools/vtable_names.py --check and the gate verify on every use. The
# class name is derived (the executable has no string for it) and its definition
# carries `/* derived name */`; a later fact renames it everywhere. Rows are added
# by the supervisor, one ruling each, never by a worker; a vtable the generated
# file names (config/vtables.<slug>.txt) must not appear here.
